# Historical evidence catalogue (2026-09-30)

Every entry is byte-pinned in [manifest.json](manifest.json). See [restore instructions](README.md).

Git entries are checked in. Archive entries may remain as local materialized copies; a fresh checkout can retrieve the verified ZIP from the persistent Sentaurus VM location in the recovery guide and restore original paths. Historical report text and numeric evidence are unchanged.
